-- [dbo].[WSspSelectOrderIndicatorWithItemsSerial] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*RZ14020325*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspSelectOrderIndicatorWithItemsSerial]
(		
/* 
	exec WSspSelectOrderIndicatorWithItemsSerial 3, 1033, 1065
*/
@UserId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------------------*/
DECLARE @IndicatorTermTxt NVARCHAR(MAX) = N'', @Qty NVARCHAR(MAX)= N''
SELECT @IndicatorTermTxt = Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 40 AND UserId = @UserId

/*---------------------------------*/

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @Qty = N'
		SELECT 
			 [OrderItemSerialID]
			,[OrderItemId]
			,(SELECT 
				(SELECT TOP(1) PrdName FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE P.ProductID = OI.ordIPrdId)
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.[OrderItemID] = OIS.[OrderItemId]) [PrdName] 
			,[SerialId]
			,[Description]     
			,[WHId]
			,(SELECT TOP (1) [WrhsName] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = OIS.[WHId]) [WrhsName] 
			,(SELECT Serial FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) OISSerial
			,(SELECT ExpDate FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) ExpDate
			,(SELECT ExpDatePersian FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) ExpDatePersian
			,(SELECT [Description] FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) [DISDescription]
			,(SELECT InfoI FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) InfoI 
			,(SELECT InfoII FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) InfoII
			,(SELECT InfoIII FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) InfoIII
			,(SELECT InfoIV FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) InfoIV 
			,(SELECT InfoV FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) InfoV 
			,(SELECT InfoVI FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) InfoVI
			,(SELECT DocNumber FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = 
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = (SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocNumber
			,(SELECT DocDate FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = 
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = 
					(SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocDate
			,(SELECT DocDateTime FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = 
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = 
					(SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocDateTime
			,(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID =
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = 
				(SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocID 
		FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
		WHERE OIS.[OrderItemId] IN ( 
									SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
									FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI 
									WHERE OI.OrderId IN(									
									SELECT O.OrderID 
									FROM WSOrder O					
								'+ @IndicatorTermTxt + ' ))	'	
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @Qty = N'
		SELECT 
			 [OrderItemSerialID]
			,[OrderItemId]
			,(SELECT 
				(SELECT TOP(1) PrdName FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] P WHERE P.ProductID = OI.ordIPrdId AND P.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+')
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.[OrderItemID] = OIS.[OrderItemId]) [PrdName] 
			,[SerialId]
			,(SELECT [Description] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials_Lang] OISLN WHERE OISLN.OrderItemSerialID = OIS.OrderItemSerialID AND OISLN.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') [Description]     
			,[WHId]
			,(SELECT TOP (1) [WrhsName] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse_Lang] WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = OIS.[WHId] AND WH.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') [WrhsName] 
			,(SELECT Serial FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) OISSerial
			,(SELECT ExpDate FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) ExpDate
			,(SELECT ExpDatePersian FROM WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId) ExpDatePersian
			,(SELECT [Description] FROM WSDocItemSerials_Lang DIS WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId AND DIS.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') [DISDescription]
			,(SELECT InfoI FROM WSDocItemSerials_Lang DIS WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId AND DIS.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') InfoI 
			,(SELECT InfoII FROM WSDocItemSerials_Lang DIS WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId AND DIS.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') InfoII
			,(SELECT InfoIII FROM WSDocItemSerials_Lang DIS WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId AND DIS.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') InfoIII
			,(SELECT InfoIV FROM WSDocItemSerials_Lang DIS WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId AND DIS.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') InfoIV 
			,(SELECT InfoV FROM WSDocItemSerials_Lang DIS WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId AND DIS.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') InfoV 
			,(SELECT InfoVI FROM WSDocItemSerials_Lang DIS WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId AND DIS.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') InfoVI
			,(SELECT DocNumber FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = 
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = (SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocNumber
			,(SELECT DocDate FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = 
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = 
					(SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocDate
			,(SELECT DocDateTime FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = 
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = 
					(SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocDateTime
			,(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID =
				(SELECT DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE  DI.DocItemId = 
				(SELECT ItemId FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials WHERE DocItemSerialId = OIS.SerialId))) DocID 
		FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
		WHERE OIS.[OrderItemId] IN ( 
									SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
									FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI 
									WHERE OI.OrderId IN(									
									SELECT O.OrderID 
									FROM WSOrder O					
								'+ @IndicatorTermTxt + ' ))	'	
/**/
END

EXECUTE (@Qty)
/*SELECT (@Qty)*/

END

Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspSelectOrdersInMode]
(		
	/* 
		exec WSspSelectOrdersInMode '', '', 0, 0, 0, 0, 1033,1065, '2017-01-01', '2023-12-05',N''    
	*/
	@FDate VARCHAR(10), 
	@TDate VARCHAR(10), 
	@UserId INT,
	@OrderId INT, 
	@CompanyId INT, 
	/*@ordNumber INT,*/
	@ordNumber BIGINT,/*RZ14000817- Change INT to BIGINT*/
	/*RZ960127 for multi language*/
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
	@FDateTime DateTime, 
	@TDateTime DateTime ,
	/*RZ960127 for multi language*/
	@ordLinkAccOrderNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL/*RZ14000301- Search by ordLinkAccOrderNumber*/
)		
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = 'SELECT ', @From NVARCHAR(MAX) = ' FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O ', @Con NVARCHAR(MAX) = ' WHERE 1=1 '
	/**/
	IF (@FDate = '-20')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		Declare @IndicatorTermTxt NVARCHAR(max) 
		SELECT @IndicatorTermTxt = Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 40 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		SET @Con = @IndicatorTermTxt + ' Order By ordDateTime Desc, ordNumber Desc'
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF ((@FDate=0) And (@TDate=0) And (@UserId=0) And (@OrderId=0) And (@CompanyId=0) And (@ordNumber=0)
		AND (ISNULL(@ordLinkAccOrderNumber,'') = '')/*RZ14000301- Search by ordLinkAccOrderNumber*/)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = ''
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@CompanyId > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND ordCompanyId = ' + CAST(@CompanyId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
							AND ordReferUserId IN 
							(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 16 )
							ORDER BY ordDateTime, ordNumber '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@OrderId > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND ordReferUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 16 )
								AND OrderId = ' + CAST(@OrderId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
								ORDER BY ordDateTime, ordNumber '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@ordNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND ordNumber = ' + CAST(@ordNumber AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
							AND ordReferUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 16 )
								ORDER BY ordDateTime, ordNumber '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (ISNULL(@ordLinkAccOrderNumber,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND ordLinkAccOrderNumber = ''' + @ordLinkAccOrderNumber + '''
							AND ordReferUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 16 )
								ORDER BY ordDateTime, ordNumber '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@OrderId = 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND CAST(ordDateTime AS DATE) BETWEEN CAST(''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATE) AND CAST(''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATE)
							AND ordReferUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 16 )

		/**/

	/**/
	/**/
	/**/

	/**/
	IF (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspSelectPreOrderIndicator]
(			
/* exec WSspSelectPreOrderIndicator '', '', '', '1', '0', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '1394',  'day', 2 */
@Date VARCHAR(8), 
@FDate VARCHAR(8), 
@TDate VARCHAR(8), 
@Month VARCHAR(10), 
@CompanyID VARCHAR(2000), 
@PreOrderNumber VARCHAR(2000), 
@StatusID VARCHAR(50),  
@pordFirstRegisterUserId VARCHAR(50),
@ReferUserId VARCHAR(50), 
/*--*/
@PrdFirstGroupId VARCHAR(2000),
@PrdSecondGroupId VARCHAR(2000),
@PrdId VARCHAR(2000),
@PreOrderOprId VARCHAR(2000),
/*--*/
@OtherFieldName VARCHAR(2000),
@OtherFieldValueLikely VARCHAR(50),
@OtherFieldValue VARCHAR(2000),
/*--*/
@rptUserId INT = NULL , 
@rptDate VARCHAR(50) = NULL , 
@rptDay VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@Mode INT,
/*ADBF9512*/
@IsVerified varchar(50),
@HaveOrder varchar(50),
/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@DateTime DateTime, 
@FDateTime DateTime, 
@TDateTime DateTime 
/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
/*RZ980710- Add Verification Workflow*/
/*RZ990207- Set null*/
,@VerificationWorkflowId varchar(50) = NULL,
@VerificationWorkflowStepId varchar(50) = NULL,
/*RZ990207-END*/
/*RZ980710- END*/
/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId,pordDeliveryTermId*/
@PordDeliveryTermId VARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@PordTypeId VARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
/*RZ14000116- End*/
/*RZ14000130- Add pordTotalAmount,pordSellerId*/
@PordTotalAmount VARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@PordTotalAmountTo VARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@PordSellerId VARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
/*RZ14000130- End*/
@SaleCenterId VARCHAR(50) = NULL,/*NKR14010709*/
/*RZ14010815- Add pordExpireDate*/
@FExpireDateTime DATETIME = NULL, 
@TExpireDateTime DATETIME = NULL, 
/*RZ14010815- End*/
@HaveLinkAcc VARCHAR(50) = NULL,/*RZ14020325- Add fields and parameters*/
/*RZ14020629- Add parameters*/
@PrdColorId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@PrdSize NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@PrdDesign NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL
/*RZ14020629- End*/
)
As
BEGIN

DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(max), @Term NVARCHAR(max), @TermExtra NVARCHAR(MAX)='',
		@QryI VARCHAR(max), @TermI VARCHAR(max)
		
/*--
--
--
--*/

SET @Term = ' 
WHERE pordReferUserId
IN
(
SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UserId
UNION 
SELECT LinkedUserId
FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers LU INNER JOIN dbo.Users U 
     		ON (U.UserId = LU.LinkedUserId)
WHERE LinkedTypeId = 15
AND CrmUserId = '+CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10))
+
') ' 
/*--*/
SET @TermI = @Term	
/*--*/
IF @Date <> ''		
BEGIN 	
	SET @Term = @Term +
	/*' AND pordDate = ' + @Date */
	/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
	' AND CAST(CAST(pordDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) = ''' + CAST(CAST(@DateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
IF ISNULL(@FDate, '') <> '' AND ISNULL(@TDate, '') <> '' /*NKR14010511- @FDate <> '' AND @TDate <> ''*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	/*' AND pordDate BETWEEN ' + @FDate + ' AND ' + @TDate*/
	/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
	' AND pordDateTime BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
/**/
/*RZ14010815- Add pordExpireDate*/
IF (@FExpireDateTime IS NOT NULL AND @TExpireDateTime IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +	
	' AND pordExpireDateTime BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FExpireDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@TExpireDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
/*RZ14010815- End*/
/**/
IF ISNULL(@Month, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @Month > '0'*/
BEGIN
	/*SET @Term = @Term + ' AND SUBSTRING(pordDate, 1, 6)  IN ( ' + @Month  +')'*/
	/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
	if(@LanguageID is not null and @LanguageID != 1065)
	begin
		SET @Term = @Term +
		/*' AND MONTH(pordDateTime) = ( ' + SUBSTRING(@Month, 5, 2)  +')'*/
		' AND LEFT(CONVERT(varchar, pordDateTime,112),6) =  ' + @Month /*RZ930202 Edit for seach by month*/
	end
	else
	begin
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND SUBSTRING(pordDate, 1, 6)  IN ( ' + @Month  +')'
	end
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END	
/**/
IF ISNULL(@PreOrderNumber, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @PreOrderNumber > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordNumber IN ( ' + @PreOrderNumber +')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@CompanyID, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @CompanyID > '0'*/
BEGIN
	if @CompanyID = '-20'
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
		SET @txt = 'CRMCompany'
		SELECT @txt = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
		AND TypeId = 1	
		/*--*/
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND pordCompanyId IN (  	
		SELECT CompanyId FROM ( ' + 
		@txt
		+ ' )t )'	
		
		/*-----------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020411- Modify in report from company*/		
		IF(ISNULL(@txt,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				CompanyId INT,
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
			DECLARE @CmpRptQry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@CmpRptPrjId INT = NULL,@CmpRptPrsId INT = NULL
			SET @CmpRptQry = ' 
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				CompanyId INT,
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
	
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			SELECT DISTINCT
				CompanyId,
				ProjectId,
				CompanyPersonId
			FROM ( ' +  @txt + ' )t 
				
			SELECT CompanyId,ProjectId,CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl'
						
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			EXEC (@CmpRptQry)			
			/*--------------------------------*/
			SET @CmpRptPrjId = (SELECT TOP(1) ProjectId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(ProjectId,0) <> 0)
			SET @CmpRptPrsId = (SELECT TOP(1) CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) <> 0)
			/*--------------------------------*/
			
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrjId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND  ISNULL(pordProjectId,0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(ProjectId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t ) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrsId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND ISNULL(pordCompanyPersonId,0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14020411- End*/
		/*-----------------------------------*/			   
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordCompanyId IN ( ' + @CompanyID  +')'
	END	
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END 
/**/
IF ISNULL(@StatusID, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @StatusID > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordStatusId IN ( ' + @StatusID +')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@pordFirstRegisterUserId, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @pordFirstRegisterUserId > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordFirstRegisterUserId IN ( ' + @pordFirstRegisterUserId +')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@ReferUserId, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @ReferUserId > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordReferUserId IN ( ' + @ReferUserId +')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END 
/**/
IF (ISNULL(@SaleCenterId, '') <> '' AND ISNULL(@SaleCenterId, '') <> '0')
BEGIN
	/**/
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordSaleCenterId IN ( ' + @SaleCenterId +')'
	/**/
	SET @TermI = @Term	
	/**/

	/*RZ14020503- End*/ '
	/*RZ14021009- Add fields*/
	SET @QryI = @QryI + ' 
		,(SELECT [prjInquiryNum] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] Pr WHERE Pr.ProjectID = PO.pordProjectId) [prjInquiryNum]
		,(SELECT [prjNumRef] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] Pr WHERE Pr.ProjectID = PO.pordProjectId) [prjNumRef]
		,(SELECT [prjNumR] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] Pr WHERE Pr.ProjectID = PO.pordProjectId) [prjNumR] 	
	'
	/*RZ14021009- End*/
	SET @QryI = @QryI + N' FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO INNER JOIN dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI 
		ON (PO.PreOrderID = POI.PreOrderId) 
	'
end
 
/*--
--
--
--
--*/
/*SET @Qry = @Qry + @Term + ' Order By pordDate Desc, pordNumber Desc '*/
/*--
--*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF @Mode = 1 /*--Indicator*/
BEGIN
EXECUTE ( @Qry + @Term + ' Order By pordDate Desc, pordNumber Desc ')
/*--*/
DELETE CrmsysDBTempInfo 
WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
AND TypeId = 30
/*--*/
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
	UserId,
	TypeId,
	TXT
) VALUES ( @rptUserId, 30, @Term) 
/*--*/
END
ELSE
IF @Mode = 2 /*--ListPreOrder*/ /*Filter PreOrder with Product*/
BEGIN
EXECUTE ( @QryI + @TermI +  ' ORDER BY pordCompanyId, pordNumber, pordDate, pordIPrdId ')
END
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspSelectPreOrderIndicatorSum]
(			/*-- exec WSspSelectPreOrderIndicatorSum '', '13890612', '13910612', 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 */
@Date VARCHAR(8), 
@FDate VARCHAR(8), 
@TDate VARCHAR(8), 
@Month VARCHAR(10), 
@CompanyID VARCHAR(2000), 
@PreOrderNumber VARCHAR(2000), 
@StatusID VARCHAR(50),  
@pordFirstRegisterUserId VARCHAR(50),
@ReferUserId VARCHAR(50), 
/*--*/
@PrdFirstGroupId VARCHAR(2000),
@PrdSecondGroupId VARCHAR(2000),
@PrdId VARCHAR(2000),
@PreOrderOprId VARCHAR(2000),
/*--*/
@OtherFieldName VARCHAR(2000),
@OtherFieldValueLikely VARCHAR(50),
@OtherFieldValue VARCHAR(2000),
/*--*/
@rptUserId INT = NULL , 
@rptDate VARCHAR(50) = NULL , 
@rptDay VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
/*ADBF9512*/
@IsVerified varchar(50),
@HaveOrder varchar(50),
/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@DateTime DateTime, 
@FDateTime DateTime, 
@TDateTime DateTime 
/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
/*RZ980710- Add Verification Workflow*/
/*RZ990207- Set null*/
,@VerificationWorkflowId varchar(50) = NULL,
@VerificationWorkflowStepId varchar(50) = NULL,
/*RZ990207- End*/
/*RZ980710- END*/
/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId,pordDeliveryTermId*/
@PordDeliveryTermId VARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@PordTypeId VARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
/*RZ14000116- End*/
/*RZ14000130- Add pordTotalAmount,pordSellerId*/
@PordTotalAmount VARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@PordTotalAmountTo VARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@PordSellerId VARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
/*RZ14000130- End*/
@SaleCenterId VARCHAR(50) = NULL,/*NKR14010709*/
/*RZ14010815- Add pordExpireDate*/
@FExpireDateTime DATETIME = NULL, 
@TExpireDateTime DATETIME = NULL, 
/*RZ14010815- End*/
@HaveLinkAcc VARCHAR(50) = NULL,/*RZ14020325- Add fields and parameters*/
/*RZ14020629- Add parameters*/
@PrdColorId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@PrdSize NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@PrdDesign NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL
/*RZ14020629- End*/
)
As
BEGIN

DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(max), @Term NVARCHAR(max)
/*--
--
--
--*/
SET @Term = ' 
WHERE pordReferUserId
IN
(
SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UserId
UNION 
SELECT LinkedUserId
FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers LU INNER JOIN dbo.Users U 
     		ON (U.UserId = LU.LinkedUserId)
WHERE LinkedTypeId = 15
AND CrmUserId = '+CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10))
+
') ' 
/*--*/
IF ISNULL(@Date, '') <> ''	/*--Date*//*NKR14010511- @Date*/
BEGIN 	
	/**/
	/*' AND pordDate = ' + @Date */
	/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
	/*' AND pordDateTime = ''' + CAST(@DateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''*/
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND CAST(CAST(pordDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) = ''' + CAST(CAST(@DateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	/**/
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@FDate, '') <> '' AND ISNULL(@TDate, '') <> '' /*--BetweenDate*//*NKR14010511- @FDate <> '' AND @TDate <> ''*/
BEGIN
	/**/
	/*' AND pordDate BETWEEN ' + @FDate + ' AND ' + @TDate*/
	/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
	SET @Term = @Term +' AND pordDateTime BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	/**/
END
/**/
/*RZ14010815- Add pordExpireDate*/
IF (@FExpireDateTime IS NOT NULL AND @TExpireDateTime IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +' AND pordExpireDateTime BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FExpireDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@TExpireDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
END
/*RZ14010815- End*/
/**/
IF ISNULL(@Month, '0') <> '0'	/*--Month*//*NKR14010511- @Month > '0'*/
BEGIN
	/**/
	/*SET @Term = @Term + ' AND SUBSTRING(pordDate, 1, 6)  IN ( ' +  @Month +')'  */
	/*ESP960123 for multi language*/
	if(@LanguageID is not null and @LanguageID != 1065)
	begin
		SET @Term = @Term +

		' AND LEFT(CONVERT(varchar, pordDateTime,112),6) =  ' + @Month /*RZ960202 Edit for seach by month*/
	end
	else
	begin
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND SUBSTRING(pordDate, 1, 6)  IN ( ' + @Month  +')'
	end
	/**/
END	
/**/
IF ISNULL(@PreOrderNumber, '0') <> '0' /*--PreOrderNumber*//*NKR14010511- @PreOrderNumber > '0'*/

	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordNumber IN ( ' + @PreOrderNumber +')' 
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@CompanyID, '0') <> '0'	/*--Company*//*NKR14010511- @CompanyID > '0'*/
BEGIN
	/**/
	if @CompanyID = '-20'
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
		SET @txt = 'CRMCompany'
		SELECT @txt = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
		AND TypeId = 1

		' AND pordCompanyId IN (  	
		SELECT CompanyId FROM ( ' +

		+ ' )t )'
		
		/*-----------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020411- Modify in report from company*/		
		IF(ISNULL(@txt,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				CompanyId INT,
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
			DECLARE @CmpRptQry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@CmpRptPrjId INT = NULL,@CmpRptPrsId INT = NULL
			SET @CmpRptQry = ' 
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				CompanyId INT,
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
	
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			SELECT DISTINCT
				CompanyId,
				ProjectId,
				CompanyPersonId
			FROM ( ' +  @txt + ' )t 
				
			SELECT CompanyId,ProjectId,CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl'
						
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			EXEC (@CmpRptQry)			
			/*--------------------------------*/
			SET @CmpRptPrjId = (SELECT TOP(1) ProjectId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(ProjectId,0) <> 0)
			SET @CmpRptPrsId = (SELECT TOP(1) CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) <> 0)
			/*--------------------------------*/
			
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrjId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND  ISNULL(pordProjectId,0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(ProjectId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t ) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrsId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND ISNULL(pordCompanyPersonId,0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14020411- End*/
		/*-----------------------------------*/			
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordCompanyId IN ( ' + @CompanyID  +')'
	END	
	/**/
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@StatusID, '0') <> '0'	/*--Status*//*NKR14010511- @StatusID > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordStatusId IN ( ' + @StatusID +')'
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@SaleCenterId, '') <> '' AND ISNULL(@SaleCenterId, '') <> '0'/*NKR14010709*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordSaleCenterId IN ( ' + @SaleCenterId +')'
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@pordFirstRegisterUserId, '0') <> '0'	/*--FirstRegisterUser*//*NKR14010511- @pordFirstRegisterUserId > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordFirstRegisterUserId IN ( ' + @pordFirstRegisterUserId +')'

IF ISNULL(@ReferUserId, '0') <> '0' /*--ReferUser*//*NKR14010511- @ReferUserId > '0'*/

/**/

BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND PreOrderID IN 
							( 
							SELECT PreOrderId 
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems pordi INNER JOIN dbo.WSProducts p ON pordi.pordIPrdId = 
						p.ProductID
											INNER JOIN WSProductSecondGroup psg ON 
						p.prdgroupid = prdSecondGroupId
							WHERE psg.FirstGroupId IN ( ' +  @PrdFirstGroupId   + ') )'	/*--FirstGroupId*/		
		
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@PrdSecondGroupId, '0') <> '0'	/*--PrdSecondGroup*//*NKR14010511- @PrdSecondGroupId > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +' AND PreOrderID IN 
							( 
							SELECT PreOrderId 
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems pordi INNER JOIN dbo.WSProducts p ON pordi.pordIPrdId =

							WHERE p.PrdGroupID IN ( ' + @PrdSecondGroupId + ') )'	/*--SecondGroupId*/
END
/**/
IF ISNULL(@PrdId, '0') <> '0'	/*--PrdId*//*NKR14010511- @PrdId > '0'*/
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND PreOrderID IN 
						( 
						SELECT PreOrderId FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
						WHERE pordIPrdId IN ( ' +  @PrdId  + ') ) ' /*--PrdId	*/

/**/
IF ISNULL(@PreOrderOprId, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @PreOrderOprId > '0'*/	/*--PreOrderOprId*/

/**/
/*--OtherField*/
IF ISNULL(@OtherFieldName, '0') <> '0' /*NKR14010511- @OtherFieldName <> '0'*/
BEGIN
	IF @OtherFieldValue = '-1'	
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND (   REPLACE(CAST(  PO.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' IS NULL OR PO.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' = '''') '
	ELSE 
	IF @OtherFieldValue = '-2'	
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND IsNull(    REPLACE(CAST(  PO.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740),  NCHAR(1610)) '+
		' , '''') <> '''' '
	ELSE
	IF @OtherFieldValueLikely = 1
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND     REPLACE(CAST(  PO.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' Like (''%''+ ''' + @OtherFieldValue + ''' + ''%'')'
	ELSE 
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND      REPLACE(CAST(  PO.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' = '+ '''' + @OtherFieldValue + '''' 
END
/*--*/
/*RZ980710- Add Verification Workflow*/
IF CAST(ISNULL(@VerificationWorkflowId, '0') AS INT) > 0 /*NKR14010511- @VerificationWorkflowId > '0'*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF CAST(ISNULL(@IsVerified, '0') AS INT) <> 0 /*NKR14010511- @IsVerified > '0'*/
	BEGIN
		IF @IsVerified = '1' /*===> not expired*/
			SET @Term = @Term +	' AND PO.[PreOrderID] IN (					
			SELECT [ObjectId]      
			FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
			WHERE [ObjectType] = 10 /*PreOrder*/
			AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowStepId +' 
			AND [VerificationRequestId] IN (
				SELECT [VerificationRequestId]      
				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
				WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId + ' 
				AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
				)
			) '
		
		ELSE IF @IsVerified = '-1'/*expired*/			
			/*RZ980730- Change condition in verificationWorkflow and add field*/
			SET @Term = @Term +	'AND PO.[PreOrderID] IN (
				SELECT [VerificationReqObjectId]      
				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
				WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10 /*PreOrder*/
				AND [VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId +'
				AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
				AND [VerificationRequestId] NOT IN (SELECT [VerificationRequestId]
														FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS LEFT JOIN
														(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRDI 
															WHERE  VRDI.[ObjectType] = 10) VRD
															ON VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = VRD.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]
															WHERE VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId +'
															AND VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowStepId +')						
			 )'							
		/*--*/		
	END
	ELSE 
	BEGIN
		/*RZ980730- Change condition in verificationWorkflow and add field*/
		SET @Term = @Term +	' AND PO.[PreOrderID] IN (					
		SELECT [VerificationReqObjectId]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] VR LEFT JOIN
		(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRDI 
		WHERE  VRDI.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]/*NKR14030212*/ = ' + @VerificationWorkflowStepId +' AND VRDI.[ObjectType] = 10) VRD
		ON VR.[VerificationRequestId] = VRD.[VerificationRequestId]
		WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10 /*PreOrder*/
		AND VR.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId + '
		AND VRD.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowStepId + '/*NKR14030212*/
		AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
		) '
		/**/		
	END	

/**/
END
ELSE IF CAST(ISNULL(@VerificationWorkflowId, '0') AS INT) <= 0 /*NKR14010511- @VerificationWorkflowId <= '0'*/
BEGIN 
	/*ADBF9512*/
	IF CAST(ISNULL(@IsVerified, '0') AS INT) <> 0 /*NKR14010511- @IsVerified > '0'*/
	BEGIN
		if @IsVerified = '1' 
			SET @Term = @Term +	' AND pordIsVerified = 1 '
		else

			SET @Term = @Term +	' AND ISNULL(pordIsVerified, 0) = 0 '
	END	

END
/*RZ980710- END*/

/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId,pordDeliveryTermId*/

	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND pordDeliveryTermId IN ( ' + @PordDeliveryTermId +')'

BEGIN

		SET @Term = @Term +

/*RZ14020325- End*/

IF (ISNULL(@PrdColorId, '0') <> '0')
BEGIN
	/**/
	SET @Term = @Term + N' AND Po.PreOrderID IN (SELECT OII.[PreOrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OII WHERE OII.[pordIPrdColorId] IN (' + @PrdColorId + ')) '	
	/**/
END
/**/
IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(@PrdSize, ''))) <> '')
BEGIN
	/**/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND Po.PreOrderID IN (SELECT OII.[PreOrderID] 
														  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OII 
														  WHERE OII.[pordIPrdId] IN (SELECT PI.ProductID 
																				   FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PI
																				   WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(PI.[PrdSize], ''''))) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
																						=
																						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(''' + @PrdSize + ''', ''''))) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))														  
																					)) '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND Po.PreOrderID IN (SELECT OII.[PreOrderID]

																				   AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(PI.[PrdSize], ''''))) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
																						=
																						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(''' + @PrdSize + ''', ''''))) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))														  
																					)) '
	/**/
	END		
	/**/
END
/**/
IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(@PrdDesign, ''))) <> '')

																					)) '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN

														  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OII 
														  WHERE OII.[pordIPrdId] IN (SELECT PI.ProductID 
																				   FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PI
																				   WHERE PI.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '
																				   AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(PI.[PrdDesign], ''''))) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
																						=
																						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(''' + @PrdDesign + ''', ''''))) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))														  
																					)) '
	/**/
	END		
	/**/
END
/*RZ14020629- End*/
/*--------------------------*/
/**/
/*--------------------------*/
SET @Qry = ' Select 
				SUM(pordItemsAmount) SUMpordItemsAmount, 
				SUM(pordDiscount) SUMpordDiscount, 
				SUM(pordSumAmount) SUMpordSumAmount,
				SUM(pordTax) SUMpordTax,
				SUM(pordTransportation) SUMpordTransportation,
				SUM(pordRound) SUMpordRound,
				SUM(pordTotalAmount) SUMpordTotalAmount
				/*RZ14020325- Add fields and parameters*/
				,SUM([pordItemsQty]) SumpordItemsQty
				,SUM([pordItemsDiscount]) SumpordItemsDiscount
				,SUM([pordItemsTax]) SumpordItemsTax
				/*RZ14020325- End*/	

				/*RZ14020327- Add fields*/
				,(SELECT SUM(ISNULL(OII.pordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(OII.pordIDiscountSum,0))
					FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OII 
					WHERE OII.PreOrderId IN(SELECT PreOrderID FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO ' +@Term + ')
					AND ISNULL(OII.pordINotInSum, 0) = 0) [SumpordItemsPriceWithoutDiscount]
				,(SELECT SUM(ISNULL(OII.pordIPriceSum,0))
					FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OII 
					WHERE OII.PreOrderId IN(SELECT PreOrderID FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO ' +@Term + ')
					AND ISNULL(OII.pordINotInSum, 0) = 0) [SumpordItemsPriceWithDiscount] 
				/*RZ14020327- End*/

				
			FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO '
/*--*/
SET @Qry = @Qry + @Term  
/*--*/
EXECUTE (@Qry)
/**/
END

Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspSelectPreOrdersInMode]
(
	/* 
		exec [dbo].[WSspSelectPreOrdersInMode] @FDate='14010101',@TDate='14010613',@UserId=3,@PreOrderId=0,@CompanyId=0,@pordNumber=0,
				@LanguageID=1033,@DefaultLanguageID=1065,@FDateTime='2022-03-21',@TDateTime='2022-09-04',@pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber=N''
	*/
	@FDate VARCHAR(10), 
	@TDate VARCHAR(10), 
	@UserId INT,
	@PreOrderId INT, 
	@CompanyId INT, 
	/*@pordNumber INT,*/
	@pordNumber BIGINT,/*RZ14000817- Change INT to BIGINT*/
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
	@FDateTime DateTime, 
	@TDateTime DateTime,
	@pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL/*RZ14000301- Search by pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber*/
)		
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = 'SELECT ', @From NVARCHAR(MAX) = ' FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO ', @Con NVARCHAR(MAX) = ' WHERE 1=1 '
	/**/
	/**/
	IF (@FDate = '-20')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @IndicatorTermTxt NVARCHAR(MAX)
		SELECT @IndicatorTermTxt = Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 30 AND UserId = @UserId 
		/**/
		SET @Con = @IndicatorTermTxt
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF ((@FDate=0) And (@TDate=0) And (@UserId=0) And (@PreOrderId=0) And (@CompanyId=0) And (@pordNumber=0)
		AND (ISNULL(@pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber,'') = ''))
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Cols = ''
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@CompanyId > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND pordCompanyId = ' + CAST(@CompanyId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
							AND pordReferUserId IN 
							(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 15 )
							ORDER BY pordDate, pordNumber, pordNumberRevision/*RZ990821- Add fields*/ '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@PreOrderId > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND pordReferUserId IN 
							(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 15 )
							AND PreOrderId = ' + CAST(@PreOrderId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
							ORDER BY pordDate, pordNumber,pordNumberRevision /*RZ990821- Add fields*/ '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@pordNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND pordNumber = ' + CAST(@pordNumber AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
							AND pordReferUserId IN 
							(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 15 )
							ORDER BY pordDate, pordNumber,pordNumberRevision /*RZ990821- Add fields*/ '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (ISNULL(@pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND [pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber] = ''' + @pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber + '''
							AND pordReferUserId IN 
							(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 15 )
							ORDER BY pordDate, pordNumber,pordNumberRevision '
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@PreOrderId = 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Con = @Con + ' AND CAST(PO.pordDateTime AS DATE) BETWEEN CAST(''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATE)  AND CAST(''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATE) 
							AND pordReferUserId IN 
							(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION

	/**/
	/**/
	/**/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)

		/**/

				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP 
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = pordCompanyPersonId) CompanyPerson,
			(SELECT CCP.prsMobile FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = pordCompanyPersonId) CompanyPersonMobile, 
			(SELECT prsEmail FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = PordCompanyPersonId) CompanyprsEmail, 
			(SELECT prsEmailII FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = PordCompanyPersonId) CompanyprsEmailII, 
			/*RZ960905- Join prjtitle + prjpostfix*/
			/*(SELECT prjTitle + ISNULL('' '' + prjPostFix,'''') FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang prj WHERE prj.ProjectID = pordProjectId and prj.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') ProjectTitle, */

			/*RZ14020605- Add prjremind to prjTitle*/
			(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [prjTitle] + ISNULL ('' '' + [prjPostFix],'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang] PRJLN WHERE PRJLN.ProjectID = prj.ProjectID AND PRJLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '),'''')
				+ ISNULL('' '' + REPLACE(((FORMAT(prjRemindDateTime, ''yyyy/MM/dd'', ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [LanguageCollation] FROM [dbo].[Languages] L WHERE L.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) +'),''en-US'')))),''.'',''/'') ,'''')
			FROM dbo.CrmProjects prj 
			WHERE prj.ProjectID = pordProjectId ) ProjectTitle, 
			/*RZ14020605- End*/

			(SELECT PreOrderStatus FROM dbo.WSPreOrderStatus_Lang sln WHERE sln.PreOrderStatusId = pordStatusId and sln.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') pordStatus,
			(SELECT G.Guaranty FROM dbo.WSGuaranty_Lang G WHERE G.GuarantyID = pordGuarantyId and G.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') Guaranty,
			(SELECT PC.PayCondition FROM dbo.WSPayCondition_Lang PC WHERE PC.PayConditionID = pordPayConditionId and PC.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') PayCondition, 
			(SELECT DT.Deliverytime FROM dbo.WSDeliveryTime_Lang DT WHERE DT.DeliveryTimeID = pordDeliveryTimeId and DT.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') DeliveryTime,
			(SELECT CD.CreditDuration FROM dbo.WSCreditDuration_Lang CD WHERE CD.CreditDurationID = pordCreditDurationId and CD.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') CreditDuration, 
			(SELECT Title FROM dbo.PrintFormat_Lang fln WHERE fln.PrintFormatID = pordPrintFormatId and fln.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') PrintFormat, 
			(SELECT ISNULL(U.LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = PO.UserID and U.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') UserName,				
			(SELECT ISNULL(U.LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = pordFirstRegisterUserId and U.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') FirstRegisterUserName,
			(SELECT ISNULL(U.LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = pordReferUserId and U.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') ReferUserName,
			(SELECT ISNULL(U.LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = pordVerifierId and U.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') VerifierFName,
			(SELECT O.PreOrderOpr FROM dbo.WSPreOrderOpr_Lang O WHERE O.PreOrderOprId = PO.PreOrderOprId and O.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') PreOrderOpr  
			,PO.pordVerificationWorkFlowId,PO.[pordExpireDate],PO.[pordExpireDateTime]/*RZ980707- Add Fields*/
			,PO.[pordLinkAccPreOrderId],PO.[pordLinkAccPreOrderNumber]/*RZ980720- Add fields*/
			,PO.pordLinkAccPreOrderIdStr /*RZ14000728- Add pordLinkAccPreOrderIdStr*/ 
			/*RZ990821- Add fields*/
			,[pordCurrencyUnitId]'	
		SET @Cols = @Cols + '
			,(SELECT CurrencyUnit FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits_Lang] CU WHERE CU.CurrencyUnitID = PO.[pordCurrencyUnitId] AND CU.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [pordCurrencyUnit]
			,[pordNumberRevision]
			/*RZ991213- Add pordNumRef And remove pordNumberPreFix,pordNumberPostFix*/
			/*,[pordNumberPreFix]
			,[pordNumberPostFix]*/
			,[pordNumRef]
			/*RZ991213- End*/
			/*RZ990821- End*/
			/*RZ990915- Add Last Revision and fields*/
			,[pordDeliveryTermId]
			,(SELECT DeliveryTerm FROM [dbo].[WSDeliveryTerms_Lang] DT WHERE DT.DeliveryTermId = PO.[pordDeliveryTermId] AND DT.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')[pordDeliveryTerm]
			/*,[pordCostSheetId]
			,(SELECT ctshNumber FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheets] CTSH WHERE CTSH.CostSheetId = [pordCostSheetId]) [pordCostSheetNumber]*/
			,[pordCostSheetIds] /*RZ991203- Change pordCostSheetId*/
			,[pordParentPreOrderId]
			,(SELECT ISNULL(MAX(WPO.pordNumberRevision), 0)
				FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WPO
				WHERE WPO.pordNumber =  PO.pordNumber
				AND WPO.FPId =  PO.FPId)[LastPordNumberRevision]
			/*RZ990915- End*/
			/*RZ991119- Add pordDeliveryEndDate */
			,[pordDeliveryEndDate],[pordDeliveryEndDateTime],[pordDeliveryEndTime] 
			/*RZ991119- End */
			/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId*/
			,[pordTypeId]
			,(SELECT [PreOrderType] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderTypes_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[PreOrderTypeId] = PO.[pordTypeId] AND PT.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [pordType]
			/*RZ14000116- End*/
			/*RZ14000130- Add fields*/
			,[pordDefWHId]
			,(SELECT [WrhsName] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse_Lang] WH WHERE WH.[WareHouseID] = PO.[pordDefWHId] AND WH.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [pordDefWrhsName]
			,[pordDefPrdPriceGroupId]
			,(SELECT [ProductPriceGroupName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup_Lang] PPG WHERE PPG.[ProductPriceGroupID] = PO.[pordDefPrdPriceGroupId] AND PPG.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [pordDefPrdPriceGroup]
			,[pordSellerId]
			,(SELECT ISNULL(U.LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.FName, '''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = PO.[pordSellerId] AND U.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') pordSellerName
			,[pordEmailUsersInfoId]
			,(SELECT ISNULL([euNikeName],'''') + ISNULL(''['' + [euNikeEmail] + '']'','''') FROM [dbo].[EmailUsersInfo] EUI WHERE EUI.[EmailUsersInfoId] = PO.[pordEmailUsersInfoId]) [pordEmailUsersInfo]
			,[pordEmailUserDefaultSettingId]
			,(SELECT [eudTitle] FROM [dbo].[EmailUserDefaultSetting] EUDS WHERE EUDS.[EmailUserDefaultSettingId] = PO.[pordEmailUserDefaultSettingId]) [pordEmailUserDefaultSetting]				
			/*RZ14000130- End*/
			/*RZ14000706- Add pordSaleCenterId*/
			,[pordSaleCenterId]
			,(SELECT SaleCenterName FROM [dbo].[WSSaleCenters_Lang] SC WHERE SC.SaleCenterId = PO.[pordSaleCenterId] AND SC.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [pordSaleCenter]
			/*RZ14000706- End*/
			/*RZ14010629- Add pordDefPayTypeId*/
			,[pordDefPayTypeId]
			,(SELECT [ordpayType] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPaymentTypes_Lang] OPT WHERE OPT.[OrderPaymentTypeId] = PO.[pordDefPayTypeId] AND OPT.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [pordDefPayType]
			/*RZ14010629- End*/ 
			/*RZ14020210- Add pordDeadlineDate*/
			,[pordDeadlineDate]
			,[pordDeadlineDatePersian]
			/*RZ14020210- End*/ 
			'
		
		/*RZ14020322- Add fields*/
		SET @Cols = @Cols + ' 
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [TaxIndexItem] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderDefaultSetting] DS WHERE DS.[PreOrderSettingID] = PO.[pordDefaultSettingId]) [TaxIndexItem]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [CalculateTaxItem] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderDefaultSetting] DS WHERE DS.[PreOrderSettingID] = PO.[pordDefaultSettingId]) [CalculateTaxItem]  
		'
		/*RZ14020322- End*/
		/*RZ14021114- Add transferDate*/
		SET @Cols = @Cols + ' 
		,[pordLinkAccFirstTransferDate]
		,[pordLinkAccLastTransferDate]  
		'
		/*RZ14021114- End*/

		/*RZ14030129- Add CompanyAddress*/
		SET @Cols = @Cols + ' 
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [cmpAddress] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = pordCompanyId AND CC.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [CompanyAddress]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) Province FROM [dbo].[Province_Lang] PRO WHERE PRO.ProvinceID =
			(SELECT TOP(1) cmpProvinceId FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE C.CompanyID = pordCompanyId) AND PRO.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [CompanyProvince]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [City] FROM [dbo].[City_Lang] CTY WHERE CTY.CityID =
			(SELECT TOP(1) cmpCityId FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE C.CompanyID = pordCompanyId) AND CTY.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [CompanyCity]  
		'
		/*RZ14030129- End*/
		/**/
	END
	/**/
	/**/
	/**/
	/**/
	/**/
	/**/
	EXEC (@Cols + @From + @Con)
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspSelectStockSupply] | References permission/temp-filter machinery









/*RZ14020908*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspSelectStockSupply]
( 
/*
	exec WSspSelectStockSupply 
	@FDate ='14020908', 
	@TDate = '14020908', 
	@FDateTime = '2023-11-29', 
	@TDateTime = '2023-11-29', 
	@StockSupplyId = 0, 
	@stsupNumber = 0,	
	@UserId = 3,			
	@LanguageID = 1033,
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065
	
*/
@FDate VARCHAR(10), 
@TDate VARCHAR(10), 
@FDateTime DATETIME, 
@TDateTime DATETIME, 
@StockSupplyId INT, 
@stsupNumber BIGINT,	
@UserId INT,			
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS 
BEGIN 
/**/
/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX) = 'SELECT ', @From NVARCHAR(MAX) = ' FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] ST ', @Trems NVARCHAR(MAX) = ' WHERE 1=1 '
	, @OrderBy NVARCHAR(MAX) = ' '
	/**/
	SET @Trems = @Trems + ' AND (ST.[stsupReferUserId] = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
		 OR (ST.[stsupReferUserId] <> ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '  AND
				(
					SELECT COUNT (*)
						FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L
						WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 32
						AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
						AND L.LinkedUserId = ST.[stsupReferUserId]
				) > 0
			)		
		)  ' 		
	/**/
	IF (@FDate = '-20')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @IndicatorTermTxt VARCHAR(MAX) 
		SELECT @IndicatorTermTxt = Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 90 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		SET @Trems = @IndicatorTermTxt
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@stsupNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trems = @Trems + ' AND [stsupNumber] = ' + CAST(@stsupNumber AS VARCHAR(50))
		SET @OrderBy = ' ORDER BY [stsupDateTime], [stsupNumber] '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@StockSupplyId = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trems = @Trems + ' AND CAST([stsupDateTime] AS DATE) BETWEEN CAST(''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATE) AND CAST(''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATE) '
		SET @OrderBy = ' ORDER BY [stsupDateTime], [stsupNumber] '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@StockSupplyId > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trems = @Trems + ' AND [StockSupplyId] = ' + CAST(@StockSupplyId AS VARCHAR(50))
		SET @OrderBy = ' ORDER BY [stsupDateTime], [stsupNumber] '
	/**/
	END	
	/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			 [StockSupplyId]
			,[StockSupplyOprId]
			,'''' [StockSupplyOpr]
			,[stsupReferUserId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[stsupReferUserId]) [stsupReferUser]
			,[stsupNumber]
			,[stsupDateTime]
			,[stsupDate]
			,[stsupCompanyId]
			,[stsupCompanyPersonId]
			,[stsupProjectId] 
			'

		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			,(SELECT TOP(1) 
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) + '' '','''') +
				ISNULL(C.cmpName,'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			  WHERE C.CompanyID = [stsupCompanyId]) [CompanyName]		
			,(SELECT TOP(1) 
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CRMPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId) + '' '','''') +
				ISNULL(CP.prsFName + '' '','''') + ISNULL(Cp.prsLName,'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP
			  WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPerson]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) 				
				ISNULL(PRJ.prjTitle,'''') + ISNULL('' '' + PRJ.prjPostFix,'''') +
				+ ISNULL('' '' + REPLACE(((FORMAT(prjRemindDateTime, ''yyyy/MM/dd'', ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [LanguageCollation] FROM [dbo].[Languages] L WHERE L.[LanguageID] = PRJ.[LanguageID]),''fa-IR'')))),''.'',''/'') ,'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ
			  WHERE PRJ.ProjectId = [stsupProjectId]) [ProjectTitle]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) prsMobile FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPersonMobile]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) prsEmail FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPersonEmail]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) prsEmailII FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPersonEmailII]
			'

		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			,[stsupIsFixed]
			,[stsupIsCanceled]
			,'''' [stsupIsCanceledTitle]
			,[stsupIsVerified]
			,[stsupVerifierRequestId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[stsupVerifierRequestId]) [stsupVerifierRequest]
			,[stsupVerifierId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[stsupVerifierId]) [stsupVerifier]
			,[stsupVerifiedRegisterDate]
			,[stsupVerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
			,[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId] 
			'
			
		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			,[stsupPrintFormatId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) PF.Title FROM [dbo].[PrintFormat] PF WHERE PF.PrintFormatID = ST.[stsupPrintFormatId]) [stsupPrintFormat]
			,[stsupNote]
			,[stsupItemCount]
			,[stsupItemsQty]
			,[stsupPreOrderId] 
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[FirstRegisterUserId]) [FirstRegisterUser]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[UserId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[UserId]) [EditUser]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[GUId]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID] 
			'					
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			 [StockSupplyId]
			,[StockSupplyOprId]
			,'''' [StockSupplyOpr]
			,[stsupReferUserId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[stsupReferUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [stsupReferUser]
			,[stsupNumber]
			,[stsupDateTime]
			,[stsupDate]
			,[stsupCompanyId]
			,[stsupCompanyPersonId]
			,[stsupProjectId] 
			'

		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			,(SELECT TOP(1) 
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') + '' '','''') +
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CLN.CmpName FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID and CLN.LanguageID =' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) +'),'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			  WHERE C.CompanyID = [stsupCompanyId]) [CompanyName]		
			,(SELECT TOP(1) 
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CRMPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') + '' '','''') +
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(CPLN.prsFName + '' '','''') + ISNULL(CPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID and CPLN.LanguageID =' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '), '''')				
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP
			  WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPerson]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) 	
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(PRJLN.prjTitle,'''') + ISNULL('' '' + PRJLN.prjPostFix,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang] PRJLN WHERE PRJLN.ProjectId = PRJ.ProjectId AND PRJLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '),'''') +			
				+ ISNULL('' '' + REPLACE(((FORMAT(prjRemindDateTime, ''yyyy/MM/dd'', ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [LanguageCollation] FROM [dbo].[Languages] L WHERE L.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) +'),''en-US'')))),''.'',''/'') ,'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ
			  WHERE PRJ.ProjectId = [stsupProjectId]) [ProjectTitle]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) prsMobile FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPersonMobile]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) prsEmail FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPersonEmail]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) prsEmailII FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [stsupCompanyPersonId]) [CompanyPersonEmailII]
			'

		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			,[stsupIsFixed]
			,[stsupIsCanceled]
			,'''' [stsupIsCanceledTitle]
			,[stsupIsVerified]
			,[stsupVerifierRequestId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[stsupVerifierRequestId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [stsupVerifierRequest]
			,[stsupVerifierId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[stsupVerifierId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [stsupVerifier]
			,[stsupVerifiedRegisterDate]
			,[stsupVerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
			,[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId] 
			'
			
		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
			,[stsupPrintFormatId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) PF.Title FROM [dbo].[PrintFormat_Lang] PF WHERE PF.PrintFormatID = ST.[stsupPrintFormatId] AND PF.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [stsupPrintFormat]			
			,(SELECT TOP(1) STLN.[stsupNote] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply_Lang] STLN WHERE STLN.[StockSupplyId] = ST.[StockSupplyId] AND STLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [stsupNote]
			,[stsupItemCount]
			,[stsupItemsQty]
			,[stsupPreOrderId] 
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[FirstRegisterUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [FirstRegisterUser]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[UserId]
			,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName +'' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = ST.[UserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') [EditUser]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[GUId]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID] 
			'					
	/**/
	END
	/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*SELECT @Qry + @From + @Trems + @OrderBy*/
	EXEC (@Qry + @From + @Trems + @OrderBy)
/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

/*RZ14020920*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspSelectStockSupplyIndicator]
(			
	/* 
		exec WSspSelectStockSupplyIndicator 
		@DateTime = NULL,
		@FromDateTime = NULL,
		@ToDateTime = NULL,
		@CompanyID = '0', 
		@StsupNumber = '0', 
		@ReferUserId = '0', 
		@WarehouseId = '0', 
		@PrdId = '0',
		@IsVerified  = '0',
		@VerificationWorkflowId  = '0',
		@VerificationWorkflowStepId  = '0',
		@IsCanceled  = '0',
		@SupplyStatus  = '0',
		@OtherFieldName  = '0',
		@OtherFieldValueLikely  = '0',
		@OtherFieldValue  = '0',
		@rptUserId = 3, 
		@rptDate = '1402/09/20' , 
		@rptDay = N'2shanbe',
		@LanguageID = 1033,
		@DefaultLanguageID = 1065
	*/

@DateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@FromDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@ToDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@CompanyID VARCHAR(2000), 
@StsupNumber VARCHAR(2000), 
@ReferUserId VARCHAR(2000), 
@WarehouseId VARCHAR(2000), 
@PrdId VARCHAR(2000),
@IsVerified varchar(2000),
@VerificationWorkflowId varchar(2000),
@VerificationWorkflowStepId varchar(2000),
@IsCanceled varchar(2000),
@SupplyStatus varchar(2000),
@OtherFieldName VARCHAR(2000),
@OtherFieldValueLikely VARCHAR(50),
@OtherFieldValue VARCHAR(2000),
@rptUserId INT, 
@rptDate VARCHAR(50) = NULL , 
@rptDay VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Term NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @TermExtra NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
		@QryI VARCHAR(MAX) = '', @TermI VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
		
/*---------------------------------------------*/
SET @Term = @Term + ' AND (ST.[stsupReferUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
		 OR (ST.[stsupReferUserId] <> ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '  AND
				(
					SELECT COUNT (*)
						FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L
						WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 32
						AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
						AND L.LinkedUserId = ST.[stsupReferUserId]
				) > 0
			)		
		) 
		 AND STI.[stsupIPrdId] NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								 FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								 WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )'
/**/
SET @TermI = @Term

		AND TypeId = 1

				CompanyId INT,

			)

				CompanyId INT,
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
	
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			SELECT DISTINCT
				CompanyId,
				ProjectId,
				CompanyPersonId
			FROM ( ' +  @txt + ' )t 
				
			SELECT CompanyId,ProjectId,CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl'
						
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			EXEC (@CmpRptQry)			
			/*--------------------------------*/
			SET @CmpRptPrjId = (SELECT TOP(1) ProjectId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(ProjectId,0) <> 0)
			SET @CmpRptPrsId = (SELECT TOP(1) CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) <> 0)
			/*--------------------------------*/

			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +

				@txt
				+ ' )t ) '
			/**/
			END

			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrsId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND ISNULL([stsupCompanyPersonId],0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END				
		/*-----------------------------------*/			   
	END
	ELSE

	SET @TermI = @Term	
END

						THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
								ISNULL(CC.[cmpName],'''')))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')

							)				
					END)CompanyName

		 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP 
		 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = ST.[stsupCompanyPersonId]) Person
		,[stsupProjectId]

								 ),0)) <= 0)
			   THEN (CAST(''0'' AS FLOAT))
			   ELSE (ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0) 
					  - ISNULL((SELECT 
									 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 		
								 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]

								 ),0))
		  END) [RemainedQuantity]

	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] STI ON (ST.StockSupplyId = STI.StockSupplyId)

	SET @Qry = @Qry + '
		SELECT DISTINCT
		(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.userID = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [rptUserName]
		,' + @rptDate + ' [rptDate]
		,''' + @rptDay + ''' [rptDay]
		/**/
		,ST.[StockSupplyId]
		,[StockSupplyOprId]
		,[stsupReferUserId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.userID = [stsupReferUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupReferUser]
		,[stsupNumber]
		,[stsupDateTime]
		,[stsupDate]
		,[stsupCompanyId]
		,(SELECT 
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
							ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(CLN.[cmpName],'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = CC.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')))
					ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +								
								ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(CPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCp.CompanyPersonID AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')
						FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
						WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
						)				
				END)CompanyName				
		 FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC
		 WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) Company 
	'

	SET @Qry = @Qry + '
		,[stsupCompanyPersonId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL(( SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] CPT  WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId  AND CPT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '), '''' ) +'' ''+ 
			 ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(CPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCp.CompanyPersonID AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') 
		 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP 
		 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = ST.[stsupCompanyPersonId]) Person
		,[stsupProjectId]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(prjTitle,'''') + ISNULL('' '' + prjPostFix,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang] Pr Where Pr.ProjectID = ST.[stsupProjectId] AND Pr.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') Project
		,(SELECT ISNULL(CC.CmpPreTel, '''') + CC.CmpTel FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) CompanyTel
		,(SELECT TOP (1) CC.[cmpNumber] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) CompanyNumber
		,(SELECT TOP (1) CC.[cmpCode] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) CompanyCode
		,(SELECT TOP (1) CC.cmpNationalCode FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) CompanyNationalCode
		,(SELECT TOP (1) CC.cmpSpecialPoint FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId] AND CC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') CompanySpecialPoint
		,(SELECT TOP (1) Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang cu WHERE cu.CountryId = (SELECT CC.cmpCountryId FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) AND cu.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ')CompanyCountry
		,(SELECT TOP (1) Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang pr WHERE pr.ProvinceId = (SELECT CC.cmpProvinceId FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) AND pr.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ')CompanyProvince
		,(SELECT TOP (1) City FROM dbo.City_Lang ci WHERE ci.cityId = (SELECT CC.cmpcityId FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) AND ci.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ')CompanyCity		
		,(SELECT TOP (1) CC.CmpAddress FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId] AND CC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') CompanyAddress 
		,(SELECT TOP (1) CC.CmpZipcode FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) CompanyZipcode 
		,(SELECT TOP (1) CC.cmpPostBox FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = ST.[stsupCompanyId]) CompanyPostBox 
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [prsNationalCode] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE CompanyPersonID = ST.[stsupCompanyPersonId]) PersonNationalCode
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [prsMobile] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE CompanyPersonID = ST.[stsupCompanyPersonId]) PersonMobile 		
	'

	SET @Qry = @Qry + '		
		,[stsupIsFixed]
		,[stsupIsCanceled]
		,[stsupIsVerified]
		,[stsupVerifierRequestId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.userID = [stsupVerifierRequestId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupVerifierRequest]
		,[stsupVerifierId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.userID = [stsupVerifierId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupVerifier]
		,[stsupVerifiedRegisterDate]
		,[stsupVerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
		,[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId]
		,(SELECT [VerificationWorkFlowTitle] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlows_Lang] VW WHERE VW.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ST.[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId] AND VW.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupVerificationWorkFlow] 
	'

	SET @Qry = @Qry + '
	 ,(SELECT TOP 1
			(SELECT [VerificationWorkFlowStepTitle] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps_Lang] VWS WHERE VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = VRD.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] AND VWS.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') 	                   
		  FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD
		  WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] IN (SELECT [VerificationRequestId]
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] VR
												WHERE VR.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ST.[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId]
												AND [VerificationReqObjectId] = ST.[StockSupplyId]
												AND [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45
												AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0) 
		  ORDER BY [VerificationRequestId] DESC,[VerifyDateTime] DESC ) [LastVerificationWorkFlowStep]
		,(SELECT TOP 1
				(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = VRD.[VerifyUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ')
			FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD
			WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] IN (SELECT [VerificationRequestId]
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] VR
												WHERE VR.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ST.[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId]
												AND [VerificationReqObjectId] = ST.[StockSupplyId]
												AND [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45
												AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0) 
			ORDER BY [VerificationRequestId] DESC,[VerifyDateTime] DESC ) [LastVerifyUser]
			,(SELECT TOP 1 [VerifyDateTime]
			FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD
			WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] IN (SELECT [VerificationRequestId]
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] VR
												WHERE VR.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ST.[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId]
												AND [VerificationReqObjectId] = ST.[StockSupplyId]
												AND [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45
												AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0) 
			ORDER BY [VerificationRequestId] DESC,[VerifyDateTime] DESC ) [LastVerifyDateTime]  
	'

	SET @Qry = @Qry + '
	   ,[stsupPrintFormatId]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) PF.Title FROM [dbo].[PrintFormat_Lang] PF WHERE PF.PrintFormatID = ST.[stsupPrintFormatId] AND PF.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupPrintFormat]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) STLN.[stsupNote] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply_Lang] STLN WHERE STLN.[StockSupplyId] = ST.[StockSupplyId] AND STLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupNote]
		,[stsupItemCount]
		,[stsupItemsQty]
		,[stsupPreOrderId] 
		,ST.[FirstRegisterUserId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.userID = ST.[FirstRegisterUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [FirstRegisterUser]
		,ST.[FirstRegisterDate]
		,ST.[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
		,ST.[UserId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.userID = ST.[UserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [EditUser]
		,ST.[RegisterDate]
		,ST.[RegisterDatePersian]
		/**/
		,[StockSupplyItemId]		
		,[stsupIPrdId]		
		,(SELECT TOP(1) PrdName FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] P WHERE ProductID = [stsupIPrdId] AND P.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIPrdName]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) PrdLatinName FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE ProductID = [stsupIPrdId]) [stsupIPrdLatinName]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) PrdCode FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE ProductID = [stsupIPrdId]) [stsupIPrdCode]	
		,(SELECT TOP(1) FGName 
		  FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup_Lang] FG 
		  WHERE PrdFirstGroupId = (SELECT FirstGroupId 
							       FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG 
								   WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = (SELECT TOP(1) PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE ProductID = [stsupIPrdId])) AND FG.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIPrdFGName]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) SGName 
		  FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup_Lang] SG 
		  WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = (SELECT TOP(1) PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE ProductID = [stsupIPrdId]) AND SG.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ')[stsupIPrdSGName]			  
		,(SELECT TOP (1) P.[PrdDesign] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] P WHERE P.[ProductID] = [stsupIPrdId] AND P.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [PrdDesign]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) P.[PrdSize] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] P WHERE P.[ProductID] = [stsupIPrdId] AND P.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [PrdSize]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) P.[PrdColorId] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE P.[ProductID] = [stsupIPrdId]) [PrdColorId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [Color] FROM [dbo].[WSProductColor_Lang] PC WHERE PC.[ProductColorID] = 
			(SELECT TOP (1) P.[PrdColorId] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE P.[ProductID] = [stsupIPrdId]) AND PC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [PrdColor]			
		,[stsupIQuantity]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [stsupIDescription] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems_Lang] STILN WHERE STILN.[StockSupplyItemId] = STI.[StockSupplyItemId] aND STILN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIDescription]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [stsupIQuantityDesc] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems_Lang] STILN WHERE STILN.[StockSupplyItemId] = STI.[StockSupplyItemId] aND STILN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIQuantityDesc]
		,[stsupIPrdUnitId]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [Unit] FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits_Lang] U WHERE ProductUnitID = [stsupIPrdUnitId] AND u.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIPrdUnit]
		,[stsupIOrderView]
		,[stsupIFCR]
		,[stsupIFCRI]		
		,[stsupIWHId]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) WH.WrhsName FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse_Lang] WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = [stsupIWHId] AND WH.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') AS [stsupIWrhsName]
	'

	SET @Qry = @Qry + '
	    ,[stsupITracing]
		,[stsupIPrdUnitIdI]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [Unit] FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits_Lang] U WHERE ProductUnitID = [stsupIPrdUnitIdI] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIPrdUnitI]
		,[stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult]
		,(CASE WHEN (ISNULL([stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult],0) <> 0 )
			   THEN 
					(CAST((FORMAT(ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0) / ISNULL([stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult],0), ''000.000'')) AS FLOAT))						
			   ELSE NULL
		 END) [stsupIPrdUnitIRate]
		,[stsupIPrdColorId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [Color] FROM [dbo].[WSProductColor_Lang] PC WHERE PC.[ProductColorID] = [stsupIPrdColorId] AND PC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIPrdColor]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [stsupIPrdNickname] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems_Lang] STILN WHERE STILN.[StockSupplyItemId] = STI.[StockSupplyItemId] aND STILN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') [stsupIPrdNickname]
		,[stsupIPreOrderId]
		,[stsupIPreOrderItemId]		
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [pordIQuantity] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.[PreOrderItemId] = [stsupIPreOrderItemId]) [pordIQuantity]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [pordIFCR] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.[PreOrderItemId] = [stsupIPreOrderItemId]) [pordIFCR]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [pordIFCRI] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.[PreOrderItemId] = [stsupIPreOrderItemId]) [pordIFCRI]		
		/**/
		,[stsupIBuyOrderIds]		
		,(SELECT 
			 [BuyOrderID]
			,[BuyOrderOprId]
			,(SELECT TOP (1) [Name] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderOpr_Lang] BOPR WHERE BOPR.[BuyOrderOprID] = BO.[BuyOrderOprId] AND BOPR.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ')[BuyOrderOpr]
			,[bordNumber]
			,[bordDate]
			,[bordDateTime]			
		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] BO
		WHERE BO.[BuyOrderID] IN((SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderIds],'','') WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0))
		FOR JSON PATH) [stsupIBuyOrders] 
		/**/
		,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds]
		,(SELECT 
			 [BuyOrderItemId]
			,[BuyOrderId]			
			,[bordIQuantity]			
			,[bordIFCR]			
			,[bordIFCRI]			
		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI
		WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'','') WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
		FOR JSON PATH) [stsupIBuyOrderItems]	
	'
	
	SET @Qry = @Qry + '
		,[stsupILinkAccWHIdStr]
		,(SELECT 
			 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 		
		 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
		 WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'','') WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
		 ) [BuyOrderItemQuantity]
		,(CASE WHEN ((ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0) 
					  - ISNULL((SELECT 
									 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 		
								 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
								 WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'','') WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
								 ),0)) <= 0)
			   THEN (CAST(''0'' AS FLOAT))
			   ELSE (ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0) 
					  - ISNULL((SELECT 
									 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 		
								 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
								 WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'','') WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
								 ),0))
		  END) [RemainedQuantity]

	   FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] ST
	   INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] STI ON (ST.StockSupplyId = STI.StockSupplyId)	 
	'			
/**/
END
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
EXECUTE ( @Qry + @Term + ' ORDER BY [stsupDateTime] DESC, [stsupNumber] DESC,[stsupIPrdId] ')
/*SELECT ( @Qry + @Term + ' ORDER BY [stsupDateTime] DESC, [stsupNumber] DESC,[stsupIPrdId] ')*/
/*-----------------------------*/
DELETE CrmsysDBTempInfo 
WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
AND TypeId = 90
/*--*/
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
	UserId,
	TypeId,
	TXT
) VALUES ( @rptUserId, 90, @Term) 
/*-----------------------------*/

/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
