-- [dbo].[WSspSelectStockSupplyIndicatorSum] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*RZ14020920*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspSelectStockSupplyIndicatorSum]
(			
/*	
	exec WSspSelectStockSupplyIndicatorSum 
	@DateTime = NULL,
	@FromDateTime = NULL,
	@ToDateTime = NULL,
	@CompanyID = '0', 
	@StsupNumber = '0', 
	@ReferUserId = '0', 
	@WarehouseId = '0', 
	@PrdId = '0',
	@IsVerified  = '0',
	@VerificationWorkflowId  = '0',
	@VerificationWorkflowStepId  = '0',
	@IsCanceled  = '0',
	@SupplyStatus  = '0',
	@OtherFieldName  = '0',
	@OtherFieldValueLikely  = '0',
	@OtherFieldValue  = '0',
	@rptUserId = 3, 
	@rptDate = '1402/09/20' , 
	@rptDay = N'2shanbe',
	@LanguageID = 1033,
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065

*/
@DateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@FromDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@ToDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@CompanyID VARCHAR(2000), 
@StsupNumber VARCHAR(2000), 
@ReferUserId VARCHAR(2000), 
@WarehouseId VARCHAR(2000), 
@PrdId VARCHAR(2000),
@IsVerified varchar(2000),
@VerificationWorkflowId varchar(2000),
@VerificationWorkflowStepId varchar(2000),
@IsCanceled varchar(2000),
@SupplyStatus varchar(2000),
@OtherFieldName VARCHAR(2000),
@OtherFieldValueLikely VARCHAR(50),
@OtherFieldValue VARCHAR(2000),
@rptUserId INT, 
@rptDate VARCHAR(50) = NULL , 
@rptDay VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Term NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @TermExtra NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
		
/*---------------------------------------------*/
SET @Term = @Term + ' AND (ST.[stsupReferUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
		 OR (ST.[stsupReferUserId] <> ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '  AND
				(
					SELECT COUNT (*)
						FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L
						WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 32
						AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
						AND L.LinkedUserId = ST.[stsupReferUserId]
				) > 0
			)		
		)  
		 AND STI.[stsupIPrdId] NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								 FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								 WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )'
/*--*/
IF (@DateTime IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN 	
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND CAST(CAST([stsupDateTime] AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) = ''' + CAST(CAST(@DateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''	
END
/**/
IF (@FromDateTime IS NOT NULL AND @ToDateTime IS NOT NULL) 
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND [stsupDateTime] BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FromDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@ToDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''		
END
/**/
IF (ISNULL(@StsupNumber, '0') <> '0')
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND [stsupNumber] IN ( ' + @StsupNumber +')'	
END
/**/
IF (ISNULL(@CompanyID, '0') <> '0') 
BEGIN
	IF @CompanyID = '-20'
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
		SET @txt = 'CRMCompany'
		SELECT @txt = TXT
		FROM [dbo].[CrmsysDBTempInfo]
		WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
		AND TypeId = 1	
		/*--*/
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND ISNULL([stsupCompanyId],0) IN (  	
		SELECT CompanyId FROM ( ' + 
		@txt
		+ ' )t )'	
		
		/*-----------------------------------*/				
		IF(ISNULL(@txt,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				CompanyId INT,
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
			DECLARE @CmpRptQry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@CmpRptPrjId INT = NULL,@CmpRptPrsId INT = NULL
			SET @CmpRptQry = ' 
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				CompanyId INT,
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
	
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			SELECT DISTINCT
				CompanyId,
				ProjectId,
				CompanyPersonId
			FROM ( ' +  @txt + ' )t 
				
			SELECT CompanyId,ProjectId,CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl'
						
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(CompanyId,ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			EXEC (@CmpRptQry)			
			/*--------------------------------*/
			SET @CmpRptPrjId = (SELECT TOP(1) ProjectId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(ProjectId,0) <> 0)
			SET @CmpRptPrsId = (SELECT TOP(1) CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) <> 0)
			/*--------------------------------*/			
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrjId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND  ISNULL([stsupProjectId],0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(ProjectId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t ) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrsId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND ISNULL([stsupCompanyPersonId],0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END				
		/*-----------------------------------*/			   
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ISNULL([stsupCompanyId],0) IN ( ' + @CompanyID  +')'
	END			
END 
/**/
IF (ISNULL(@ReferUserId, '0') <> '0')
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND [stsupReferUserId] IN ( ' + @ReferUserId +')'	
END 
/**/
IF (ISNULL(@WarehouseId, '0') <> '0')
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND [stsupIWHId] IN ( ' +  @WarehouseId   + ')  ' 			 	
END
/**/
IF (ISNULL(@PrdId, '0') <> '0')
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND [stsupIPrdId] IN ( ' +  @PrdId   + ')  ' 			 	
END
/**/
IF (ISNULL(@OtherFieldName, '0') <> '0') 
BEGIN	
	IF @OtherFieldValue = '-1'	
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND (   REPLACE(CAST(  ST.' + @OtherFieldName + ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' IS NULL OR ST.' + @OtherFieldName + ' = '''') '
	ELSE 
	IF @OtherFieldValue = '-2'	
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND IsNull(    REPLACE(CAST(  ST.' + @OtherFieldName + ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740),  NCHAR(1610)) '+
		' , '''') <> '''' '
	ELSE
	IF @OtherFieldValueLikely = 1
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND     REPLACE(CAST(  ST.' + @OtherFieldName + ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' Like (''%''+ ''' + @OtherFieldValue + ''' + ''%'')'
	ELSE 
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND      REPLACE(CAST(  ST.' + @OtherFieldName + ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' = '+ '''' + @OtherFieldValue + '''' 	
END
/**/
IF CAST(ISNULL(@VerificationWorkflowId, '0') AS INT) > 0 
BEGIN
/**/	
	IF CAST(ISNULL(@IsVerified, '0') AS INT) <> 0 
	BEGIN
		IF @IsVerified = '1' /*===> not expired*/
			SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ST.[StockSupplyId] IN (					
			SELECT [ObjectId]      
			FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
			WHERE [ObjectType] = 45 /*StockSupply*/
			AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowStepId +' 
			AND [VerificationRequestId] IN (
				SELECT [VerificationRequestId]      
				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
				WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId + ' 
				AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
				)
			) '
		
		ELSE IF @IsVerified = '-1'/*expired*/
			
			/*Change condition in verificationWorkflow and add field*/
			SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	'AND ST.[StockSupplyId] IN (
				SELECT [VerificationReqObjectId]      
				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
				WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] =  45 /*StockSupply*/
				AND [VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId +'
				AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
				AND [VerificationRequestId] NOT IN (SELECT [VerificationRequestId]
														FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS LEFT JOIN
														(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRDI 
															WHERE  VRDI.[ObjectType] = 45) VRD
															ON VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = VRD.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]
															WHERE VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId +'
															AND VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowStepId +')						
			 )'								
	END
	ELSE 
	BEGIN		
		/*Change condition in verificationWorkflow and add field*/
		SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ST.[StockSupplyId] IN (					
		SELECT [VerificationReqObjectId]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] VR LEFT JOIN
		(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRDI 
		WHERE  VRDI.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowStepId +' AND VRDI.[ObjectType] = 45) VRD
		ON VR.[VerificationRequestId] = VRD.[VerificationRequestId]
		WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45 /*StockSupply*/
		AND VR.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId + '
		AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
		) '		
	END	
/**/
END
ELSE IF ISNULL(@VerificationWorkflowId, 0) <= 0 
BEGIN 	
	IF CAST(ISNULL(@IsVerified, '0') AS INT) <> 0
	BEGIN
		IF @IsVerified = '1' 
			SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ISNULL([stsupIsVerified],0) = 1 '
		ELSE 
		IF @IsVerified = '-1'
			SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ISNULL([stsupIsVerified], 0) = 0 '		
	END		
END
/**/
IF CAST(ISNULL(@IsCanceled, '0') AS INT) <> 0
BEGIN
	IF @IsCanceled = '1' 
		SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ISNULL([stsupIsCanceled],0) = 1 '
	ELSE 
	IF @IsCanceled = '-1'
		SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ISNULL([stsupIsCanceled],0) = 0 '	
END	
/**/
IF CAST(ISNULL(@SupplyStatus, '0') AS INT) <> 0
BEGIN
	IF @SupplyStatus = '1' 
	BEGIN
		SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0) <= 
											ISNULL((SELECT 
												 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 				
											FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
											WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,''0'') AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](STI.[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'',''))),0)'
	END
	
	ELSE 
	IF @SupplyStatus = '-1'
	BEGIN
		SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +	' AND ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0) > 
											ISNULL((SELECT 
												 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 				
											FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
											WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,''0'') AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](STI.[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'',''))),0)'
	END
	/*--*/	
END	
/**/
/*---------------------------*/
SET @Term  = @Term + @TermExtra
/*---------------------------*/
SET @Qry = @Qry + '
	SELECT 
		 (SUM(ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0))) [SumStsupIQuantity]
		,(SUM(ISNULL([BuyOrderItemQuantity],0))) [SumBordItemQuantity]
		,(SUM(ISNULL([RemainedQuantity],0))) [SumRemainedQuantity]
	FROM(
	SELECT 
		ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0) [stsupIQuantity]
		,(SELECT (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity], 0)))
		   FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
		   WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata, 0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds], '','') WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata, 0) AS INT) <> 0) ) [BuyOrderItemQuantity]
		,(CASE WHEN ((ISNULL([stsupIQuantity], 0) - ISNULL((SELECT (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity], 0)))
															FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
															WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata, 0) AS INT) 
																					  FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds], '','') 
																					  WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata, 0) AS INT) <> 0) ),0)) <= 0) 
				THEN (CAST(''0'' AS FLOAT))
				ELSE (ISNULL([stsupIQuantity], 0) - ISNULL((SELECT (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity], 0)))
															FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
															WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata, 0) AS INT) 
																					  FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds], '','') 
																					  WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata, 0) AS INT) <> 0) ),0))
			END) [RemainedQuantity]		                                            
	FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] ST
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] STI ON (ST.StockSupplyId = STI.StockSupplyId) 
	' + @Term + '

	) AS TMP

'
/*--------------------------*/
SET @Qry = @Qry  
/*--*/
EXECUTE (@Qry)
/*SELECT (@Qry)*/
/**/
END







GO
